## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent identity (yellow and navy palette, same title bar, footer and logo) and a logical route from offering to services, approach, proof, team, deal credentials, case studies and testimonials. The case study slides (21 to 24) use a clear metrics strip above three text columns, and the metric slides (6, 7) are easy to read. The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway. Slides 19 and 20 are sparse cards with a duplicated Sukham entry, and slide 18 leaves most of the page empty.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent header, footer and logo system, the metrics strip layout on the case study slides (21 to 24), and the number tiles on slides 6 and 7.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

**What would change the score:** I would add a takeaway headline to each logo wall (for example the count of investors by type) and group the case study and credentials slides so the proof section is shorter and easier to scan.

_Confidence high. 27 of 27 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is a corporate profile from Key Venture, a financial services firm that advises early and growth stage startups on raising capital. It opens with a title slide and an offering statement describing the firm's advisory and fund management arms. The middle sections cover the services offered (transaction advisory, fund management and portfolio management, with stage, ticket size and investor targets for each), the reasons to choose the firm, and its step-by-step approach to investment advisory across seven stages. It then presents deal metrics, investor coverage, lists of investors in recent transactions, the core team and advisors, a set of deal credentials, detailed case studies, and client testimonials. It closes with contact details for the two founders. Slides worth studying include the services overview on slide 3, which uses a consistent three-column layout for each service line, the process diagram on slide 5, and the case study template on slide 21, which lays out deal value, service, investors, industry and date in a grid before the narrative sections.

## Questions about this deck

**What services does Key Venture offer and what ticket sizes does its deck list?**

Slide 3 of the Key Venture deck lists Transaction Advisory with a ticket size of USD 2mn +, and Fund Management including an Angel Syndicate with a ticket size of USD 0.5mn to 0.7mn. The same slide lists an Angel Fund with a fund size of USD 15mn and a ticket size of USD 1mn to 1.5mn, and Portfolio Management for family offices and UHNIs.

**How many slides does the Key Venture corporate presentation have and how is it structured?**

The Key Venture deck has 27 slides. Slide 3 covers the services, slide 4 the reasons to choose the firm, and slide 5 the seven-stage approach to investment advisory.

**What investor coverage does Key Venture report in its deck?**

Slide 7 of the Key Venture deck reports 400+ VCs, 100+ Family Offices, 50+ Corporate VC or Strategic Investors, 500+ active HNIs, and 100+ Overseas Investors. It also lists Canada, USA, UK, Hong Kong, Singapore, India and Australia as coverage locations.

**What milestones does Key Venture state in its deck?**

Slide 6 of the Key Venture deck reports 22 transactions closed across 12 startups, $40M in total funds raised, and 8 sectors covered. It also cites 11 ongoing mandates and a return of ~35x generated for investors.

### Slide 4

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

**Text**

Why Choose us?
Why should Key Venture be your banker of choice?
Why do investors trust Key Venture for startup investments?
Advisory team with over 25+yrs of experience in startup investment and deal making
Wide investor coverage with deep rooted connects across asset class such as VCs, PEs, family offices and HNIs
Wide HNI and family office coverage who are willing to participate in bridge rounds
Team of experts who have successfully raised funds for deep tech startups and unique products which are sector creators or first in the country
Focused approach with high employee headcount to live mandate ratio, ensuring personalized support, quick turnarounds and high conversion ratio
Multiple specialized teams to support a company throughout the process including helping a founder become investor ready, preparing an investment thesis and relevant collaterals, valuation discovery, support during due diligence and negotiating investment agreements
Track record of running a structured process within tight timelines to meet funding requirements
Our investors get access to deals across stages starting from MVP stage companies to bridge rounds in companies who have raised Series B+, including pre IPO rounds
We are sector agnostic which helps investors build a diversified portfolio across sectors such as deep tech, enterprise SaaS, robotics, consumer, healthcare and lifesciences.
We only present highly filtered startups to our investors based on their investment criteria and risk appetite. Only 2-3% of the proposals we receive are presented to our investors.
On an average any startup would have gone through 3 levels of filtration before reaching our investors. This includes initial screening by our screening team, second level of filtering after a business due diligence from our business leaders followed by a final filtration from a sector specific investment team which includes industry experts from our member network.
Our business diligence includes extensive market sizing, technology deep dive, competition analysis, business and financial analysis. Hence, our investors are able to make quick and informed decisions delivering quick turnaround times.
Our vertical specific teams for due diligence and legal documentation help safeguard the investor interests.
Our deal advisory team handholds startups across future rounds of funding to ensure continued growth in our portfolio companies.
Our post investment portfolio management team ensures that the startups are functioning in the manner promised to investors such that quick decisions can be taken whenever required and no exit opportunity is missed leading to high RoI for investors.
4
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

White slide with a title at top left preceded by a yellow tab at the left edge. Below, two text columns separated by a faint vertical shadow divider at the centre; each column has a blue heading and a bulleted list. Page number box at bottom left, centred confidential footer, logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Yellow rounded pill/tab at the far left edge beside the title. Faint grey vertical gradient shadow line between the columns (about x 49.5-51.5%). Thin light grey horizontal footer line near the bottom. Small light grey square behind the page number. Key Venture logo at bottom right: a navy swoosh checkmark with a yellow curved stroke on the left, with the text 'KEY VENTURE' (KEY in navy, VENTURE in yellow) and a tiny tagline 'KEY TO SUCCESS' beneath, and a small registered mark at the top right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in dark grey #404040 Calibri Bold, about 6% of slide height. Column headings in teal-blue #125675 Calibri Bold, about 3% height. Body text in dark grey #404040 Calibri regular with key phrases in bold, about 2.2% height, with small blue #125675 round bullets. Accent yellow #F0C320. Footer text light grey #A5A5A5. No rounded cards; the divider has a soft shadow.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The seven-step approach is shown as numbered blocks arranged around a central title, with one short descriptive sentence per step, giving a clear visual sequence for a process.
- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

**Text**

Our Approach to Investment Advisory
01
02
03
04
05
06
07
Screening / Committee
Investment / Collaterals
Investors
Commercial / Negotiation
DD / Compliances
Partners
Deal Closure
Our Advisor Board does an in-depth screening & shortlisting of the companies.
Our in-house team works very closely with the promoters in making an effective Pitch deck & Financial Model.
We believe in scouting for right investors, who understand the entrepreneurs’ business and stand with promoters during thick and thin.
We handhold the startups during Term Sheet and SHA negotiations.
We give utmost importance that our clients’ meets all the statutory compliances and hand hold them during the DD process to ensure swift closure of the process.
We have industry experts, lawyers, CA, CS and DD firms as partners. KV’s in-house team works very closely with our Partners & Startups’ promoters.
We believe in long term relationships and support the startup post deal closure as well.
5
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a small yellow tab on the left edge. Seven equal-width outlined cards in a single row across the slide, each with a numbered circle straddling its top edge, a coloured header band and body text beneath, left-aligned. Footer with page number box at bottom left, centred confidentiality text and a logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Seven tall white cards with thin light-grey borders. Seven circles with thin light-grey outline and white fill, each containing a light-grey number, overlapping the top border of its card. Header bands alternate dark teal-blue and golden yellow. A rounded yellow tab at the left edge next to the title. A thin light-grey footer line along the bottom. A small grey box behind the page number. Key Venture logo bottom right: a dark teal swoosh check mark with a yellow curved stroke, with the text 'KEY VENTURE' in blue and yellow and a tiny tagline beneath.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040, bold Calibri, large (about 5.9% of slide height). Card header text white bold Calibri about 3% of height. Body text dark grey #434343 Calibri regular about 2.2% of height. Numbers light grey #D9D9D9 bold. Accents: teal #125675 and yellow #EEC217. Thin grey 1px borders, no shadows, square corners on cards and bands, circles for numbers, no gradients.

**Layout**

Title at top left with a yellow tab at the left edge. A row of five equal rounded-rectangle cards sits below the title, each with a numbered circle badge straddling its top edge. A wide rounded panel below holds 'Our success stories' with logos on the left, a grey 'Deal Details' block in the middle and a 2x2 grid of figures on the right. Footer has a page number box at bottom left, centered confidential text and a logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five white rounded cards with thin outlines (alternating blue #125675 and yellow #EEC217: cards 1, 3, 5 blue; cards 2, 4 yellow). Each card has a circular badge with a number; the badge has a thick coloured arc along its lower half, matching the card colour. A wide white rounded panel with a thin yellow outline contains a light grey (#F2F2F2) vertical block with a small dark grey right-pointing triangle at its right edge. Thin light grey horizontal rules sit under the large figures $ 2M, $10M, ~$50M and ~35x. Logos: the red 'MIKO' wordmark with a superscript 3 (client); the red script 'chiratae VENTURES' logo and the 'YourNest' logo with a black and red icon (investors); the Key Venture logo at the bottom right, with a navy check mark with a yellow swoosh and the text 'KEY VENTURE' with a tagline beneath.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040, bold Calibri-style sans-serif, about 6% of slide height. Large numerals and headings in teal-blue #125675 bold, and yellow #EEC217 for the 02 and 04 cards' figures. Body text in dark grey #404040 Calibri regular at about 3% height, with bold sector lists at about 2.6%. Thin 1px outlines, rounded corners of about 2% on cards and panel, no shadows or gradients. Footer text in light grey #A5A5A5.

**Review notes**

**Layout**

Title top-left with a yellow pill tab at the left edge. A row of five equal outlined rounded cards across the upper third, each with a numbered circle badge overlapping its top edge. A world map fills the lower two-thirds, centred, with country labels and leader lines. Footer has a slide number box at bottom-left, centred confidentiality text, and a logo at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Five rounded-rectangle cards with thin 1px outlines, alternating dark teal-blue (#125675) and yellow (#EEC217): cards 1, 3 and 5 teal, cards 2 and 4 yellow. Each has a white circular badge at the top centre, outlined in the card colour with a thicker crescent shadow along the bottom edge, containing a two-digit number. A flat world map in light grey (#E7E6E6) with country borders faintly visible. Canada, USA, UK, India, Australia, and tiny marks for Hong Kong and Singapore are filled teal (#125675). Each highlighted country has a thin teal leader line ending in a round dot beside its label: Canada and USA dots on the left of the map, UK dot above the UK, India dot below India, Hong Kong and Singapore dots to the right, Australia dot to the left. A yellow rounded pill sits to the left of the title. The Key Venture logo at bottom-right has a teal check-mark swoosh with a yellow left stroke and the text KEY VENTURE in teal and yellow with a small tagline. A thin light-grey footer rule runs along the bottom, and a light-grey square holds the page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri at about 6% of slide height. Big stats are bold Calibri at about 7%, alternating teal #125675 and yellow #EEC217. Badge numbers are bold at about 4.4%, same alternating colours. Card labels and map labels are Calibri regular, dark grey #404040, at about 3%. Footer text is light grey #A5A5A5 at about 1.7%. Cards have a corner radius of about 1% and no shadow. Flat style, no gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.

**Text**

Investors in recent transactions
VCs and / Corporates
chiratae VENTURES
Inflexor
kotak Investment Advisors

EQUANIMITY

Achieving Women Equity AWE FUNDS Capital Promoting Gender Equity
9UNICORNS

8
Strictly Private and Confidential
KEY VENTURE KEY TO SUCCEED

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a small yellow pill tab at the left edge. A large rounded-corner outlined panel fills most of the slide, with a vertical yellow label in a narrow left column separated by a thin vertical line. The right area holds a logo grid of four rows (5, 4, 6, 5 logos). Footer has page number box at bottom left, centered confidentiality text, and Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Logo grid of investor logos on white: row 1 chiratae Ventures (red script), Inflexor (dark grey with blue pixel check mark), Kotak Investment Advisors (red/blue circle emblem, red 'kotak' text), Cornerstone Venture Partners (white text on teal-blue rectangle with dark triangle), Equanimity (teal V-shaped icon over black caps). Row 2 YourNest (red square with black figure, dark text), AWE Funds (green bars above and below, blue text), 9Unicorns (blue geometric unicorn, red text), Lavni Ventures (grey caps with green/blue check mark, tagline). Row 3 ACG (red italic), RPG Ventures (blue italic RPG, orange VENTURES), DSP (black serif with teal underline), Flipkart (blue italic text with yellow shopping bag icon), Baldota (blue/green swirl), HP Hindustan Petroleum (blue-bordered square with red HP). Row 4 91 Ventures (white on blue rectangle with white swoosh), GSF (multicolour swirl, black bold letters), iHub Anubhuti-IIITD Foundation (black head profile icon), 100X (red letters with outlined X), VentureCatalysts (red flower icon, grey and red text). Key Venture logo bottom right with a navy and gold V swoosh. Yellow pill at top left. Panel outline thin yellow with large rounded top-right and bottom-right corners; thin vertical yellow line at about x=11.6%. Grey thin footer line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title dark grey #404040 in Calibri bold, about 6% of slide height. Side label yellow #EEC217 Calibri bold rotated 90 degrees. Accent yellow #EEC217 used for the pill, panel outline and divider. Footer text grey #A5A5A5, page number #404040 in a light grey box. Flat style, no shadows or gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.

**Text**

Investors in recent transactions
Family / Offices
Pravin Gandhi
(Founder Seedfund)
RPG Ventures
(Harsh Goenka)
(Anant Goenka)
SAB Holdings
(Sandeep Singhal’s family office)

(Saranam Ventures)
Nirmal Bhogilal
(Chairman & MD, Batliboi Ltd.)

(Maheshwari Family Office)
Dr. Aniruddha Malpani

Rahul Baldota
(Promoter of MSPL Ltd)
9
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a yellow tab. A large rounded-corner outlined box spans the slide width below the title, with a left label column 'Family Offices' separated by a vertical line. The right area is a 5-column by 2-row grid of circular portraits, each with a name and subtitle above and a company logo below. Footer has a page number at bottom left, a centred confidentiality note and a Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Ten circular headshot photographs in two rows of five. Row 1: Pravin Gandhi (older man with glasses and dark moustache, grey jacket, green blurred background); Harsh Goenka (bearded man with glasses, dark suit, light background); Anant Goenka (young man with dark hair, dark suit, grey background); Sandeep Singhal (smiling man in dark suit with gold tie, grey background); Karan Singh (man with dark hair in dark blue suit, light background). Row 2: BVR Mohan Reddy (man with moustache, dark suit, pink tie, green foliage); Nirmal Bhogilal (grey-bearded man, grey background); Karan Maheshwari (young smiling man with hand on chin, checked shirt); Dr. Aniruddha Malpani (smiling man with glasses on bright blue background); Rahul Baldota (man in dark suit with light blue tie, light background). Company logos beneath: row 1 – lime green '<seed fund>' wordmark, navy-and-blue RPG logo (twice), 'nexus venture partners' wordmark in dark navy with green X, red ACG logo; row 2 – dark grey CYIENT wordmark, blue-and-red BATLIBOI badge, orange-triangle 'mipl' logo, black 'Malpani Ventures' with striped lines, Baldota swirl logo in blue/green/orange. A yellow rounded tab sits at the left of the title. A thin teal outlined rounded rectangle with a vertical divider line surrounds the content. Key Venture logo (navy check mark with yellow swoosh, 'KEY VENTURE' text) at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri, about 6% of slide height. Names in Calibri regular #404040 about 3%, subtitles about 2.2%. 'Family Offices' label in bold teal #125675 about 3.7%. Box outline thin teal #125675 with large top-right and bottom-right corner radius. Yellow accent #F2C21A tab. Footer text light grey #A5A5A5. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Title at top left with a short yellow rounded bar at the left edge. Below it a large rounded-rectangle outlined panel holds a 4-column by 3-row grid of circular portraits, each with name, title and company logo centred beneath. A narrow left column inside the panel, separated by a vertical yellow line, holds the label 'UHNIs' vertically centred. Footer has a page number box at bottom left, a centred confidentiality line and the Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Twelve circular headshot photographs of men in business attire, in a 4x3 grid (row 1: Keshav R Murugesh, Lakhshmi Narayanan, Viswanath Ramachandran, Shirish Shankhe; row 2: Javed Tapia, Srikanth Karra, Kalyan Basu, Manish Bharti; row 3: Giridhar Sanjeevi (black and white), Venkataramanan Anantharaman, Ankur Agarwal, Kishor Ganji). Company logos under each: WNS (black bold wordmark with orange accent), Cognizant (blue diamond icon with dark blue wordmark), Gupshup (purple code icon with dark wordmark) plus Meta (blue infinity logo and wordmark), McKinsey & Company (dark serif wordmark in two lines), Clover Infotech (green clover with grey serif text), Mphasis (pink/blue icon with dark wordmark, tagline 'The Next Applied'), Microsoft (four-colour square logo with grey wordmark), CoreStack (red/blue icon with black wordmark), IHCL (dark navy bold wordmark), TransUnion CIBIL (teal wordmark), a black square with orange arrow logo (Clarion Technologies) under Ankur Agarwal; no logo under Kishor Ganji. Key Venture logo bottom right: navy and yellow check mark with 'KEY VENTURE' text and tagline. Thin yellow outline rounded rectangle panel with vertical divider line. Thin grey footer line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri, about 6% of slide height. Accent yellow #EEC217 for the title bar, panel outline and 'UHNIs' label (bold). Body text in grey #404040 Calibri regular at about 2% of slide height. Footer text light grey #A5A5A5. Panel has large rounded top-right and bottom-right corners, 1px yellow stroke, no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

White slide with a left-aligned title at the top, a small yellow pill tab at the far left edge beside it. Below, two columns separated by a vertical grey shadow line at the centre: Mumbai on the left (2 columns x 3 rows of logos) and Bengaluru on the right (2 columns, 5 rows with the last row holding one logo). Footer has a page number box bottom left, centred confidentiality text, and the Key Venture logo bottom right.

Logos of investor firms. **Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.

**Text**

Advisors
Pravin Gandhi
Founding Partner, Seed Fund
T. N. Hari
Author, Angel Investor, Advisor to VCs, / ex CHRO BigBasket
He is a veteran of the Indian IT industry, an entrepreneur, an angel investor and mentor to various startups and funds and board member of various public and private companies
He founded Seedfund, an early stage VC, 15 yrs ago and has contributed hugely to the growth of startup ecosystem in India since then
He has held many prestigious positions across his career such as President of the Manufacturers Association of Information Technology, member of the NASSCOM Executive Council and President and Global Trustee of The Indus Entrepreneurs (TiE)
He is currently a board member of SINE (IIT Bombay’s incubation center).
Completed his BS in Industrial Engineering from Cornell University
Hari wears different hats. He is the Co-founder of Artha School of Entrepreneurship. He is also an angel investor, an evangelist, thought leader, and influencer in the Startup ecosystem. He is an alumnus of IIT Madras and IIM Calcutta.
He has authored 8 books and also writes for the center page of Mint quite regularly. His books ‘Saying No to Jugaad', ‘From 'Pony to Unicorn', and ‘Diversity Beyond Tokenism’ have been bestsellers.
He has been part of the leadership teams at five high growth start-ups and helped shape their growth journeys and exits, including a listing on NASDAQ. Three of these went on to become Unicorns. He is also passionate about helping founders build great companies and supporting women leaders discover their potential.
Hari also contributes at a strategic level through Board positions on companies whose mission he finds inspiring and in alignment with his capabilities.
13
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Title top-left beside a yellow tab at the left edge. Two equal columns separated by a faint vertical shadow line at the centre. Each column has a circular portrait with name and role to its right, a bullet list below, and a row of logos near the bottom. Page number box at bottom-left, centred confidentiality footer, Key Venture logo at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Yellow rounded tab at the left of the title. Two circular headshot photos, each in a light grey ring: left shows an older man with grey hair, moustache and tinted glasses in a white collared shirt against a grey background; right shows a smiling bald man with glasses and dark hair on the sides, in a dark top on a light sofa. Thin yellow underlines under each name. Faint vertical divider with soft shadow. Left logo row: green '<seed fund>' wordmark, red 'NASSCOM' wordmark, blue SINE (IIT Bombay Business Incubator) logo, red and white TiE logo. Right logo row: Artha School of Entrepreneurship (navy and orange octagon emblem with blue wordmark), bigbasket (green and red 'bb' icon with wordmark), Arkam Ventures (orange sun-like icon with grey text), Fundamentum (dark grey condensed wordmark). Bottom-right Key Venture logo: navy and yellow check mark with 'KEY VENTURE' text. Thin grey line across the bottom above the footer, light grey page-number box.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

**Text**

Advisors
Safir Anand
Senior Partner & Head of Department
(Trademarks, Contractual and Commercial IP),
Anand & Anand
He is one of the leading IP advisors of India. He advises large corporations on strategy, Business models, Marketing and Commercial Insights blended with an astute understanding of the IP law that covers ; IP Protection, IP Enforcement – Civil and Criminal; Contractual IP – Agreements, Licensing, Franchising, Monetization, Strategic Advisory and Due Diligence.
He has advised clients from diverse industries including FMCG, Pharma, Software, Social Media, Food Industry, Media, Sports, Entertainment, Luxury and Fashion, Government Bodies and more
He is also known for his strong understanding of investing and has been covered by media houses and television channels
Sunil Nair
India CEO, Fireworks
Angel Investor, Mentor to various startups and a writer
Sunil Nair is the India CEO of Silicon Valley-based ‘Shoppertainment’ platform Firework. He has over 18 years of experience in the media and consumer internet space.
Prior to Firework, he was the COO at ALTBalaji leading Balaji Telefilm’s foray into the OTT space. Sunil set up the first online video distribution platform – Nautanki TV (acquired by Hungama).
In his previous stints, he has held leadership positions with One Channel Media, Reliance Jio, Star India Pvt Ltd, Brand Portrait amongst others.
He continues to be active in the start-up circle as an advisor and mentor. He writes children’s books in his spare time and three of his books are available on Amazon.
14
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Two equal columns separated by a faint vertical shadow divider at the slide centre. Title top-left with a yellow tab at the left edge. Each column has a circular portrait with name, role and underline at the right of it, a bullet list below, and logos beneath. Footer has page number box bottom-left, centred confidentiality text and Key Venture logo bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Circular portrait of Safir Anand (dark-haired man, light skin, head and shoulders) in a pale grey ring at left. Circular portrait of Sunil Nair (bald man with glasses and goatee, smiling slightly) in a pale grey ring at right. Anand & Anand logo: orange outlined letter A with small text 'ANAND & ANAND'. Right column logo row: Firework (pink-magenta infinity-loop icon with 'Firework' wordmark), ALTBalaji (black rounded square with white/orange ALT and BALAJI), Jio (red circle with white 'Jio'), Star (orange star with orange 'Star' wordmark). Key Venture logo bottom-right: navy and yellow check-mark swoosh with 'KEY VENTURE' text in navy and yellow. Short yellow rounded bar left of title. Vertical grey gradient shadow line at centre. Short yellow underlines beneath each name. Thin grey horizontal footer line.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title dark grey #404040 bold Calibri about 5.9% of slide height. Names in bold teal-blue #125675 about 3% height with yellow #E8B91A underline. Subtitles #434343 and body #404040 in Calibri about 2.2% height, with roughly 1.4 line spacing. Bullets small teal dots #125675. Hyperlink 'Firework' blue #0563C1 underlined. Footer text light grey #A5A5A5. Page number in a light grey box. No gradients except the soft divider shadow.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

**Text**

Advisors
Pratap Singh Nathani
Founder & Managing Director,
Beacon Trusteeship
Pratap Singh Nathani is an experienced banker, a serial entrepreneur and a BFSI expert. He has spent over two decades in the financial services sector where he has worked across asset classes such as retail, PSUs and large corporates before he started his entrepreneurial journey.
He started his career by distributing retail bonds/NCDs & FDs to individuals at Apple Finance and later went on to raise debt for Government Institutions via Non-SLR bonds and sell them to Provident Funds at Centrum & Darashaw. Later, he was tasked with establishing the Loan Syndication business for JM Morgan Stanley. He effectively established and led the Loan Syndications & Debt Capital Markets practices at ING Vysya Bank.
He was involved in marquee transactions such as leveraged finance for an Auto ancillary and M&A Finance for a large telecom tower firm at JM Financial (following the JM-MS split).
He is currently the Founder and Managing Director of Beacon Group, a leading financial service provider to AIFs, Mutual funds, banks and NBFCs. He is also the founder of a FinTech – LoanXpress.
15
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Title at top left with a yellow pill tab at the left edge. Below, a 3x2 grid of rounded-corner outlined cards; each has a company logo centred at top, a thin divider, a two-line description, a bullet list (Industry, Funding, Investors) and investor logos beneath. Footer has a page number box at bottom left, centred confidentiality text, and a Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Company logos: FlexifyMe (blue/grey wordmark with 'Mind + Body + Soul'), algomage (white wordmark on black rectangle), monitra health (blue wave mark above teal wordmark), ajjas (orange/yellow wordmark with pin icon), AMORQA (dark navy bold wordmark), SwitchMe (green outlined box with 'TO A BETTER FUTURE' and arrow), Chaaipani (pink and yellow speech bubbles above black wordmark). Investor logos: Flipkart (blue italic wordmark with yellow shopping bag) in the FlexifyMe card and larger in the algomage card, GSF (multicolour swirl and black text), iHub Anubhuti-IIITD Foundation (black head profile icon) twice, 91 Ventures (blue square with white wing), Lavni Ventures (grey text with green paper-plane mark), ACG (red text), AWE Funds (green bars and text), HP Hindustan Petroleum (white square with navy border, red header, red HP and blue emblem). Key Venture logo (navy and yellow check-mark swoosh with text). Thin grey card outlines with rounded corners; grey footer line; yellow rounded tab beside the title.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri, about 5.9% of slide height. Body text in Calibri regular dark grey #404040 about 2% of height, with bold labels. Bullets are small teal-blue #125675 dots. Ajjas subtitle in bold teal-blue #125675. Cards have thin light-grey #D9D9D9 outlines with rounded corners and no shadows. The footer text is light grey #A5A5A5. Accent is yellow #F2C41D.

### Slide 18

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.
- Review: Slides 19 and 20 are sparse cards with a duplicated Sukham entry, and slide 18 leaves most of the page empty.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

**Text**

Deal Credentials – Mergers & Acquisitions
Artivatic.ai | RenewBuy
Key Venture advised Artivatic.ai (an AI-enabled
InsurTech platform) in its merger with RenewBuy
(an insurance distribution platform).
Sector: InsurTech
Target: Artivatic.ai
Acquirer: RenewBuy
Artivatic's Past Investors who got an exit
Strictly Private and Confidential
18

**Layout**

Title at top left beside a yellow tab flush to the left edge. A single rounded-corner card is centred in the slide (about 34% wide) holding a heading, two logos, a divider line, a description with three bullets, an underlined sub-heading and a 3x2 grid of investor logos. Footer has a centred confidentiality line, a page number box at bottom left, and the Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Yellow rounded pill/tab at left edge beside the title. Rounded rectangle card with thin light grey outline and white fill. Artivatic.ai logo: teal/green stylised AV mark above a teal-outlined box reading ARTIVATIC.AI. RenewBuy.com logo: orange 'RenewBuy' wordmark with blue '.com' and grey tagline 'Smart Tech, Right Advice'. Thin light grey horizontal divider under the logos. Blue round bullets. Investor logos row 1: KFINTECH (blue wordmark with triangular A mark), SCALE (dark green wordmark with orange arrow square), spark10 (dark 'spark' text with orange rocket 10). Row 2: Indian Angel Network (red IAN logo), Indian Angel Network Fund (red logo), Mumbai Angels Network (white text on black bar over a green gradient box). Key Venture logo bottom right: navy and gold check-mark swoosh with the text KEY VENTURE in navy and gold. Thin grey line along the footer. Page number sits in a light grey box.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri, large (about 6% of slide height). Card heading in teal-blue #125675 bold Calibri. Body text dark grey #404040 Calibri about 2.6% of slide height, bold labels for Sector, Target and Acquirer. Bullets blue #125675. Accent yellow #F0C419. Footer text grey #A5A5A5, small. Card has thin #D9D9D9 outline, large corner radius, no visible shadow.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: slides 3, 4 and 13 to 15 carry very small, dense text that is hard to read on screen, slide 5 has seven columns with large empty lower halves and faint step numbers, and slides 8 to 12 and 16 to 20 are logo and photo walls with tiny captions and no takeaway.
- Review: Slides 19 and 20 are sparse cards with a duplicated Sukham entry, and slide 18 leaves most of the page empty.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge and trim the text on slides 4 and 13 to 15, tighten slide 5 so the cards fit their content and the step numbers are visible, and fill or merge the near-empty slides 18 to 20.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The case study opens with a labelled grid of key facts (deal value, service, round, investors, industry, date) in a sidebar before the narrative, so a reader can scan the transaction details first.

**Text**

Case Study – THB
TECHNOLOGY | HEALTHCARE | BIG DATA ANALYTICS
Deal Value
$20M
Service
Private Equity
Round Name
Series B2
Investors
Cornerstone Venture Partners, Baldotta Family Office
Industry
HealthTech | Enterprise SaaS | Big Data
Date
March 2023
About the company
THB serves large healthcare enterprises to drive digital, data, and technology adoption with their data management and big data analytics platform.
THB offers a suite of software solutions across more than 20 use cases, including patient engagement, doctor engagement, clinical customer relationship management, analytics automation, customer care automation, and real-world evidence, among other things.
About the transaction
THB raised $20Mn in equity funding in two tranches. They closed Series B2 (tranche 1) for USD 5.5Mn. Prominent investors who participated in the round – Cornerstone Venture Partners (leading Enterprise SaaS VC Fund), Baldota Family Office (family office of Narendra Baldota), Deep Kalra’s family office, Mr. Shibulal’s family office.
The funding will also allow THB to expand its product portfolio and invest in growing operations globally and accelerating growth in existing countries across South Asia, Middle East and Southeast Asia.
Role of Key Venture
Financial advisor to THB in its Series B2 round.
THB operates in a unique space which includes deep understanding of clinical diagnosis and prognosis backed by an algorithm which understands clinical markers and generates actionable insights on ways to increase revenue and optimize operations
Key Venture used its expertise in healthcare and big data to identify the unique value proposition which THB offers.
Enterprise SaaS investors appreciated this unique value proposition, which helped the company get a good valuation even in difficult macro economic conditions.
21
Strictly Private and Confidential
KEY VENTURE

### Slide 22

**Text**

Case Study – Artivatic.ai | Renewbuy
Deal Value
Undisclosed
Service
Mergers and
Acquisitions
Acquirer
RenewBuy
Target
Artivatic
Sector
Robotics | Deep
Tech | EduTech
Represented
Sell Side
Date
February
2022
About Artivatic.ai
Bangalore based AI start-up integrating technology to build
intelligent applications and solutions in the InsurTech space.
Having 6 patents and 400+ API offers, a game changing technology
is used to assist insurance companies to build personalised risk
profiles of customers, to track and understand their financial and
behavioural journeys and develop real-time intelligence based on
those patterns.
About Renewbuy
This disruptor in the InsurTech space provides choice and
convenience to customers buying health, life and motor insurance
products, delivered through a trusted network of PSOP advisors.
The company is equipped with 70,000 POS partners who have
insured more than 3 million customers across 750 cities and towns.
About the transaction
RenewBuy acquires Artivatic, taking ownership
of Artivatic’s SaaS solutions for insurance, IP and
product portfolio
RenewBuy will expand its technology solution
across the value chain
Acquisition completed by a combination of cash
payment and share swap deals. Promoters of
Artivatic received shares of RenewBuy
Artivatic’s past investors IAN, MAN, Scale,
Spark10 and KFin Services were provided a
cash exit
Role of Key Venture
Identified the synergy between Artivatic
and RenewBuy
Ran a structured process, enabling both
companies to seal the deal within 4
months
Helped leverage Artivatic’s tech
capabilities to create a win-win strategy
Generated a handsome return for
Artivatic’s past investors
22
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

White slide with left-aligned title at top and a short yellow tab at the left edge. Below is a row of seven deal-fact columns (separated by thin vertical lines) preceded by two stacked company logos, then a horizontal divider. Beneath are three text columns with icon-plus-heading headers and bullet lists; the left column holds two stacked sections. Footer has a page number box at bottom left, centred confidentiality text and a Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Artivatic.ai logo (teal AV mark with boxed 'ARTIVATIC.AI' text) above the RenewBuy logo (orange and blue wordmark with '.com' and tagline 'Smart Tech, Right Advice'). Grey line icons above each fact label: handshake, gear with wrench, businessman bust, target with arrow, factory, rocket/star with hand, calendar with check. Section icons: building (twice, for About Artivatic.ai and About Renewbuy), document with dollar sign and arrows (transaction), gear with handshake and bar chart (Role of Key Venture). Thin grey vertical separator lines with soft shadow between the three columns and between fact columns; a soft grey horizontal shadow line under the fact row; thin grey rules under each section heading. Yellow rounded tab at the top left. Key Venture logo: navy and yellow check-mark swoosh with 'KEY VENTURE' text and small tagline.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri about 5.9% of slide height. Labels in grey #404040 Calibri regular about 2.2%; values and section headings in teal-blue #125675 Calibri bold (values 2.6%, headings 3%). Body bullets Calibri regular 2.2% in #404040 with small #125675 round bullets. Accent yellow #F0C419 tab. Footer text light grey #A5A5A5. Subtle shadows on divider lines; no gradients.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Case Study – SecureThings
Deal Value
$3.5M
Service
Private Equity
Round Name
Pre-Series A
Investors
Inflexor Ventures, 9Unicorns, RPG Ventures and SAB Holdings along with UHNIs; Shirish Sankhe, Karan Maheshwari, Javed Tapia and Mahesh Bharti.
Industry
SaaS | Automotive Cybersecurity | Deep-Tech
Date
February 2022
About the company
An IP driven deep tech start-up in the automotive cybersecurity domain.
Specialised machine learning based cybersecurity solutions for all automobiles; commercial vehicles, passenger vehicles, electric vehicles and motorbike.
The company is headquartered in California, USA and has a R&D centre in Pune, India.
About the transaction
Having a foothold in India and USA, investors from India, USA and Dubai participated in the round. Investors include Inflexor Ventures, 9Unicorns, SAB Holdings and RPG ventures along with family offices of Shirish Sankhe, Karan Maheshwari, Javed Tapia and Manish Bharti.
First transaction in the automotive cybersecurity in India
Funds will be injected in product development, business growth in India and overseas market, augmenting the India and US teams and sales and marketing.
Role of Key Venture
Key Venture conducted an in-depth analysis of the sector (In India and overseas) to identify the core components of the tech stack which differentiate its company from its peers.
Attracted interest from deep-tech VC funds and veteran investors from the industry
Managed a complex investment structure across the US parent and Indian subsidiary
Handheld the during the entire due diligence and documentation process
23
Strictly Private and Confidential

### Slide 24

**Text**

Case Study – Miko
Deal Value
$2M
Service
Private Equity
Round Name
Pre-Series A
Investors
Chiratae Ventures,
YourNest, a few HNIs
Industry
Robotics | Deep
Tech | EduTech
Date
March 2018
About the company
About the transaction
Role of Key Venture
Miko is a deep tech B2C enterprise which makes emotionally intelligent companion robots for children.
Their flagship product Miko, is India’s first companion robot, capable of engaging, educating and entertaining children.
Developed by a team of educators, neuro-psychologists, content developers and engineers.
Backed by a proprietary AI algorithm Miko can adapt to different personalities, dozens of emotions and millions of topics and themes to learn from.
Miko was looking for its first round of institutional capital and long term investors who could anchor their next few rounds
Funds were raised to penetrate into modern retail in India, develop the next product line, expand into US and stabilize the supply chain from China.
This round saw participation from Chiratae Ventures (formerly known as IDG Ventures India), YourNest and a few HNIs, including existing investor Keshav Murugesh (CEO of WNS Global).
Sole financial advisor to Miko in its Pre-Series A round.
Even though the company was in early revenue stage, we positioned the company in such a way that people could understand the technological capability of the product and its impact on consumers.
Venture was positioned as a consumer growth story where a child’s likelihood to talk to a digital medium is explored due to the strong AI based emotional intelligence platform
Even though the company was offering a hardware product in a segment which did not exist in India, the unique positioning helped the company command a high valuation and attracted marquee investors who continued to lead the next 3 rounds, investing a total of $10M.
24
Strictly Private and Confidential
KEY VENTURE
KEY TO SUCCESS

**Layout**

White slide with title at top left beside a yellow tab on the left edge. Below it is a full-width row of six key-fact columns (Miko logo at left, then icon, label and bold value, separated by thin vertical dividers), then a faint shadow line, then three equal bullet-list columns with icon headings and thin underlines. Footer has a page number box at bottom left, centered confidentiality text, and Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Yellow rounded tab at the left edge beside the title. Red/coral 'MIKO' wordmark logo with a darker red shading on the left. Six grey line icons: handshake (Deal Value), gear with wrench (Service), hand holding a dollar coin (Round Name), person with money bag (Investors), factory with robot arm/buildings (Industry), calendar with check mark (Date). Thin grey vertical dividers between the fact columns, and a soft grey horizontal shadow line under the row. Three grey line icons over the headings: office building (About the company), document with dollar sign and exchange arrows (About the transaction), gear with bar chart over a handshake (Role of Key Venture). Faint vertical shadow dividers between the three columns, thin grey underlines under each heading. Blue bullet dots. ### Slide 25

**Text**

Testimonials
Sandeep Singhal, Senior Advisor, Avaana
Capital & Ex-MD, Nexus Venture Partners
Key Venture was instrumental in helping SecureThings
manage a complex investment structure across the US
parent and the Indian subsidiary given the investor
interest across both geographies.
Harsha Mundhada, Principal, Inflexor Ventures
Key Venture actively supported SecureThings during the entire
investment process. They handheld the Company during the
entire due diligence and documentation process to ensure
timely closure of the transaction.
Sunil Goel, MD, YourNest Venture Partners
Emotix (a.k.a. Miko) is a great find, they demonstrate
deep expertise and understanding in Robotics and AI. ‘Key
Venture’ led by Nidhi played the role of effectively guiding
the founders for the conclusion of the pre-series A round.
We hope Key Venture keep playing the role of matching
best finds with the right Investor in the ecosystem.”
Vishal Bajpai, Co-Founder & CEO, SecureThings
Key Venture was able to understand our tech stack and identify
the value we generate for the automotive ecosystem. Their
understanding of technology and its impact across businesses
helped us in positioning our solution correctly in front of
investors and generating interest from investors who can
understand our product and journey. They handheld us
throughout the journey. Their understanding of the challenges a
young startup faces in areas such as finance, compliance and
legal helped us close this round in a seamless manner.
25
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Title at top left with a yellow pill marker bleeding off the left edge. Below, a 2x2 grid of testimonial cards, each with a rounded-corner bracket outline, a yellow opening quote mark at top and a closing quote mark at bottom right, a rounded-square portrait at the left and name/title and quote text to its right. Footer has page number at bottom left, centered confidentiality line, and Key Venture logo at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four rounded-square headshot photos with thin light-grey borders. Top left: smiling man with grey hair and dark suit, grey background. Top right: smiling woman with dark hair in a bun, blue blouse and beige saree, light background. Bottom left: smiling man with grey hair, dark-rimmed glasses and grey blazer, white background. Bottom right: smiling man in dark suit with red tie in front of a blue TV news studio backdrop with 'WORLDWIDE BUSINESS' sign. Each card has a thin light-grey (#D9D9D9) bracket line with rounded corners at top-right and bottom-right (left cards) and at top-left and bottom-right (outline open around the quote marks). Large yellow (#EBBB14) opening and closing quotation marks per card (8 total). A yellow horizontal rounded bar at top left beside the title. Key Venture logo at bottom right: a yellow and dark-teal V swoosh with 'KEY VENTURE' text (KEY in dark blue, VENTURE in yellow) and tagline 'KEY TO SUCCESS'. A thin light-grey horizontal line runs along the footer, with a small grey box behind the page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040 bold Calibri about 5.9% of slide height. Names in bold teal-blue #125675 Calibri about 3% height, followed by regular dark grey #404040 role text at the same size. Quote body in Calibri regular #404040 about 2.2% height with generous line spacing. Accent yellow #EBBB14 for quote marks and the title bar. Footer text in light grey #A5A5A5 Calibri about 1.7% height. Thin grey 1-2px lines, rounded corners on photos (about 1% radius), no shadows or gradients.

### Slide 26

**Text**

Testimonials
Puneet Tandon, Co-Founder, Artivatic
While we have cutting-edge tech capabilities and are constantly innovating in the insurance tech space, Key Venture has a keen understanding of the Insurance landscape and helped leverage our tech capabilities to create a win-win strategy for us. This transaction with RenewBuy will help us in our quest to be the most innovative solutions provider in the insurance domain.
Layak Singh, Co-Founder, Artivatic
When we started building products for the insurance sector, the word InsurTech didn’t exist. We worked hand-in-hand with insurers to build the ecosystem. Hars Saraf has been a part of our journey, since the early days. His understanding of the InsurTech space and experience in M&A helped us generate handsome returns for our investors. This deal will help us in achieving our vision of insurance for everyone.
Sneh Vaswani, CEO & Co-Founder, Miko
Key Venture is a great partner to assist companies with early-stage fundraising. When it came to Emotix, they quickly understood the product, technology, immediate/long-term business plan and identified + positioned the company to the best investors for our journey. We thank Nidhi for their support and recommend this wonderful team to all fellow startups.
Aditya Mishra, Founder & CEO, Amorqa (Switchme)
Key Venture was very quick in grasping the transformative impact of our proprietary Technology on the Indian real estate industry. They helped us fine tune our pitch and in pitching to the right investors.
26
Strictly Private and Confidential

**Layout**

Title top-left beside a yellow tab at the left edge. Four testimonial cards in a 2x2 grid, each with a rounded square portrait at the left, a name/title heading and quote text to the right, framed by a thin grey bracket outline with yellow quote marks. Footer has page number at bottom-left, centered confidentiality text, and Key Venture logo at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four portrait photos with rounded corners and thin light grey borders: top-left a man with moustache in a sepia-toned photo wearing a dark patterned shirt; top-right a man with glasses in a dark blazer and white shirt against a light green background; bottom-left a smiling man in a blue blazer and light shirt against white; bottom-right a smiling man with glasses in a light blue striped shirt against green foliage. Each card has a large yellow opening quote mark at the top-left of its outline and a yellow closing quote mark at bottom-right. Thin grey rounded-corner bracket lines outline each card. A yellow rounded pill at the left edge beside the title. Key Venture logo at bottom-right: a navy-blue and yellow check-mark swoosh with the text KEY VENTURE in navy and yellow and a small tagline beneath. A thin grey line runs across above the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in dark grey #404040, Calibri bold, large (about 6% of slide height). Two right-aligned contact blocks occupy the right side, with a footer line and confidentiality text at the bottom centre and a logo at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Dark teal (#125675) triangle with its vertical edge on the left slide edge and its diagonal running from the top-left to about x=34% at the bottom. A semi-transparent light grey (#D8D8D8, 50%) diagonal band runs along the teal triangle's hypotenuse. A yellow (#EEC217) triangle sits in the top-left corner, about 14% wide and 42% tall, partly overlapped by the teal. A thin light grey horizontal footer line runs across the bottom at about y=97%. Key Venture logo at the bottom right: a teal swoosh tick with a yellow curved left stroke, text 'KEY VENTURE' (KEY in dark navy, VENTURE in yellow) and a tiny tagline 'KEY TO SUCCESS' beneath, with a small registered mark at the top right.

Background: White #FFFFFF. White background with a teal and yellow geometric corner motif. Title in bold yellow #EEC230 Calibri at about 5.9% of slide height. Names are bold teal #125675, roles are regular teal #125675, emails are blue #0000FF and underlined, phone numbers are dark grey #404040, all Calibri at about 3.3% of slide height. Footer text in small grey #A5A5A5 Calibri. No shadows or rounded corners.
